Definition 40.1src/Lib/Shimura/Eigenspace/EigenQuotient.lean: IsEigenQuotient
Let $x , N \in \Natural$. Let $X$, $I$ and $S$ be sets. Let $V$ be a function from elements of $I$ to module categories of $\PadicMACRoFDF {x}$. Let $\rho$ be a function from elements $i_{2}$ of $I$ to representations of $X$ and the set of carriers of $V (i_{2})$. Let $T$ be a function from elements $i_{1}$ of $I$ and elements of $S$ to ends of $\PadicMACRoFDF {x}$ and the set of carriers of $V (i_{1})$. Let $\theta$ be a function from elements of $S$ to fixed algebraic closures of $x$. Let $r$ be a monoid homomorphism from $X$ to the set of general linear groups of $\FinMACRoFDI {N}$ over the set of fixed algebraic closures of $x$. Let $F$ be a function from elements $i$ of $I$ to linear maps of the set of carriers of $V (i)$ and the set of functions from elements of $\FinMACRoFDI {N}$ to fixed algebraic closures of $x$. Then $\IsEigenQuotientMACRoBHM {i_{2}}{\rho}{i_{1}}{\theta}{N}$ is a proposition defined as the function that maps $r$, $i$ and $F$ to the proposition that for all elements $i$ of $I$, for all elements $g$ of $X$, for all carriers $x_{2}$ of $V (i)$, $\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {\matrixRepMACRoEGO {\theta}}{g}$ is equal to $N (i , \DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {i_{2} (i)}{g})$, the span of the range of the function that maps $x_{10}$ to $N (\SigmafstMACRoFDQ {x_{10}}, \SigmasndMACRoFDR {x_{10}})$ is equal to the top, for all elements $i_{4}$ of $I$, for all elements $s$ of $S$, for all carriers $x_{12}$ of $V (i_{4})$, $N (i_{4} , \rho (i_{4} , s , x_{12}))$ is equal to $\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {i_{1} (s)}{N (i_{4} , x_{12})}$ and for all sets $M$, for all elements $x_{16}$ of $\mathrm{AddCommGroup} (M)$, for all elements $x_{17}$ of $\mathrm{Module}$ applied to $M$ and the set of fixed algebraic closures of $x$, for all elements $x_{18}$ of $\mathrm{Module} (M , \PadicMACRoFDF {x})$, if $\mathrm{IsScalarTower}$ holds for $\PadicMACRoFDF {x}$, the set of fixed algebraic closures of $x$ and $M$, then for all functions $f$ from elements $x_{22}$ of $I$ to linear maps of the set of carriers of $V (x_{22})$ and $M$, if for all elements $i_{6}$ of $I$, for all elements $s_{1}$ of $S$, for all carriers $x_{21}$ of $V (i_{6})$, $f (i_{6} , \rho (i_{6} , s_{1} , x_{21}))$ is equal to $\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {i_{1} (s_{1})}{f (i_{6} , x_{21})}$, then there exists a linear map $\phi$ of the set of functions from elements of $\FinMACRoFDI {T}$ to fixed algebraic closures of $x$ and $M$, such that for all elements $i_{5}$ of $I$, for all carriers $x_{19}$ of $V (i_{5})$, $\EqtMACRoDE {\phi (N (i_{5} , x_{19}))}{f (i_{5} , x_{19})}$.
The ℚ_ℓ-linear maps F_i : V_i → ℚ̄_ℓ^N span, turn T_s into the scalar θ_s, intertwine ρ_i with r, and are universal among maps to ℚ̄_ℓ-spaces turning T_s into θ_s.
Theorem 40.2src/Lib/Shimura/Eigenspace/EigenQuotient.lean: exists_isEigenQuotient
Let $x$ be a natural number. Let $X$ be a group. Let $I$ be a finite type. Let $S$ be a set. Assume that $x$ is prime. Let $V$ be a function from elements of $I$ to module categories of $\PadicMACRoFDF {x}$ such that for all elements $i_{5}$ of $I$, $\mathrm{Module\_\_Finite}$ holds for $\PadicMACRoFDF {x}$ and the set of carriers of $V (i_{5})$. Let $\rho$ be a function from elements $i_{4}$ of $I$ to representations of $X$ and the set of carriers of $V (i_{4})$. Let $T$ be a function from elements $i_{3}$ of $I$ and elements of $S$ to ends of $\PadicMACRoFDF {x}$ and the set of carriers of $V (i_{3})$. Let $\theta$ be a function from elements of $S$ to fixed algebraic closures of $x$. Assume that $X$ is a topological space. Assume that $X$ is a topological group. Assume that for all elements $i_{2}$ of $I$, for all elements $s$ of $S$, for all elements $g_{1}$ of $X$, $T (i_{2} , s)$ commutes with $\rho (i_{2} , g_{1})$. Assume that for all elements $i_{1}$ of $I$, for all carriers $x_{6}$ of $V (i_{1})$, for all natural numbers $N_{1}$, for all linear maps $f$ of the set of carriers of $V (i_{1})$ and the set of functions from elements of $\FinMACRoFDI {N_{1}}$ to fixed algebraic closures of $x$, the function that maps $g$ to $f (\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {\rho (i_{1})}{g})$ is continuous. Then there exists a natural number $N$, such that there exists a monoid homomorphism $r$ from $X$ to the set of general linear groups of $\FinMACRoFDI {N}$ over the set of fixed algebraic closures of $x$, such that there exists a function $F$ from elements $i$ of $I$ to linear maps of the set of carriers of $V (i)$ and the set of functions from elements of $\FinMACRoFDI {N}$ to fixed algebraic closures of $x$, such that $\IsEigenQuotientMACRoBHM {\rho}{T}{\theta}{r}{F}$ and $r$ is continuous.
For finitely many continuous finite-dimensional ℚ_ℓ-representations V i of Γ with operators T commuting with Γ, there are a continuous r: Γ → GL_N(ℚ̄_ℓ) and maps F from the V i forming the largest θ-quotient (`IsEigenQuotient`).
Proof. By IsEigenQuotient
and 17 lemmas of Mathlib Submodule.Quotient.eq, LinearMap.ker_eq_top, Eq.ge, LinearMap.ext, LinearEquiv.surjective, LinearMap.cancel_right, continuous_pi, LinearMap.ext_on_range, Continuous.congr, Continuous.of_coeHom_comp, Continuous.matrix_transpose, Submodule.ker_mkQ, Submodule.subset_span, Set.range_subset_iff, Commute.eq, Submodule.span_le, map_smul
. ∎
Theorem 40.3src/Lib/Shimura/Eigenspace/EigenQuotient.lean: IsEigenQuotient.isCrystalline
Let $x , N \in \Natural$. Let $X$ be a group. Let $I$ and $S$ be sets. Assume that $x$ is prime. Let $V$ be a function from elements of $I$ to module categories of $\PadicMACRoFDF {x}$. Let $\rho$ be a function from elements $i_{3}$ of $I$ to representations of $X$ and the set of carriers of $V (i_{3})$. Let $T$ be a function from elements $i_{2}$ of $I$ and elements of $S$ to ends of $\PadicMACRoFDF {x}$ and the set of carriers of $V (i_{2})$. Let $\theta$ be a function from elements of $S$ to fixed algebraic closures of $x$. Let $r$ be a monoid homomorphism from $X$ to the set of general linear groups of $\FinMACRoFDI {N}$ over the set of fixed algebraic closures of $x$. Let $F$ be a function from elements $i_{1}$ of $I$ to linear maps of the set of carriers of $V (i_{1})$ and the set of functions from elements of $\FinMACRoFDI {N}$ to fixed algebraic closures of $x$ such that $\IsEigenQuotientMACRoBHM {\rho}{T}{\theta}{r}{F}$. Assume that $\OverGpMACRoCFV {X}{x}$. Assume that for all elements $i$ of $I$, $\IsCrystallineinMACRoFCK {\rho (i)}$. Then $\IsCrystallineMACRoBGS {\matrixRepMACRoEGO {r}}$.
Proof. By IsAdmissible.baseChange_mem_span_fixedPeriods, matrixRep, isAdmissible_of_one_tmul_mem_span
and 4 lemmas of Mathlib Submodule.eq_top_iff', AddMemClass.add_mem, TensorProduct.tmul_add, Submodule.closure_induction
. ∎
Theorem 40.4src/Lib/Shimura/Eigenspace/EigenQuotient.lean: IsEigenQuotient.isKilledBy
Let $x , N , f \in \Natural$. Let $X$ be a group. Let $I$ and $S$ be sets. Assume that $x$ is prime. Let $V$ be a function from elements of $I$ to module categories of $\PadicMACRoFDF {x}$. Let $\rho$ be a function from elements $i_{4}$ of $I$ to representations of $X$ and the set of carriers of $V (i_{4})$. Let $T$ be a function from elements $i_{3}$ of $I$ and elements of $S$ to ends of $\PadicMACRoFDF {x}$ and the set of carriers of $V (i_{3})$. Let $\theta$ be a function from elements of $S$ to fixed algebraic closures of $x$. Let $r \in \MonoidHomMACRoHP {X}{(\MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {\FinMACRoFDI {N}}{\mathrm{PadicAlgCl} (x)})}$. Let $F$ be a function from elements $i_{2}$ of $I$ to linear maps of the set of carriers of $V (i_{2})$ and the set of functions from elements of $\FinMACRoFDI {N}$ to fixed algebraic closures of $x$ such that $\IsEigenQuotientMACRoBHM {\rho}{T}{\theta}{r}{F}$. Let $s$ be a finite set of $\Natural$. Let $m$ be a function from $\Natural$ to $\Integer$. Let $d$ be a function from $\Natural$ to $\Natural$. Let $t$ be a function from $\Natural$ to $S$. Assume that $\OverGpMACRoCFV {X}{x}$. Assume that for all elements $i_{1}$ of $I$, $\IsCrystallineinMACRoFCK {\rho (i_{1})}$. Let $g \in X$ such that $\IsWeilPairMACRoBPS {\mathrm{OverGp\_\_toGp\_\_app} (g)}{f}$ and for all elements $i$ of $I$, for all tensor products $y$ of $\BcrisMACRoON$ and the set of carriers of $V (i)$, $\FinsetSum {s}{k_{1}}{(\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {m (k_{1})}{\mathrm{LinearMap\_\_baseChange\_\_app} (\BcrisMACRoON , \mathrm{LinearMap\_\_restrictScalars\_\_in\_9\_10} (\PadicIntMACRoFDG {x}, \mathrm{ModuleCat\_\_Algebra\_\_instModuleCarrier} , \mathrm{ModuleCat\_\_Algebra\_\_instModuleCarrier} , T (i , t (k_{1}))), \mathrm{linearFrob\_\_app} (\rho (i), g ^ {d (k_{1})}, d (k_{1})\times f , y))})}$ is equal to $0$. Then $\IsKilledByMACRoBJS {\matrixRepMACRoEGO {r}}{g}{f}{\FinsetSum {s}{k}{(\PolynomialCappMACRoGI {(m (k)\times \theta)}\times \PolynomialXMACRoGJ ^ {d (k)})}}$.
Proof. By IsCrystalline.isPotCrys, IsWeilPair.pow, linearFrob_pow, IsEigenQuotient.isCrystalline, baseChange_linearFrob, linearFrob
and 11 lemmas of Mathlib TensorProduct.AlgebraTensorModule.ext, Submodule.eq_top_iff', Polynomial.C_mul_X_pow_eq_monomial, map_add, map_mul, TensorProduct.tmul_add, Polynomial.eval₂_finsetSum, mul_add, SMulCommClass.smul_comm, LinearMap.baseChange_tmul, Submodule.closure_induction

Theorem 40.5src/Lib/Shimura/Eigenspace/EigenQuotient.lean: IsEigenQuotient.exists_word_kill_of_jump_eq_zero
Let $x$ and $N$ be natural numbers. Let $X$ be a group. Let $I$ be a finite type. Let $S$ be a set. Assume that $x$ is prime. Let $V$ be a function from elements of $I$ to module categories of $\PadicMACRoFDF {x}$ such that for all elements $i_{7}$ of $I$, $\mathrm{Module\_\_Finite}$ holds for $\PadicMACRoFDF {x}$ and the set of carriers of $V (i_{7})$. Let $\rho$ be a function from elements $i_{6}$ of $I$ to representations of $X$ and the set of carriers of $V (i_{6})$. Let $T$ be a function from elements $i_{5}$ of $I$ and elements of $S$ to ends of $\PadicMACRoFDF {x}$ and the set of carriers of $V (i_{5})$. Let $\theta$ be a function from elements of $S$ to fixed algebraic closures of $x$. Let $r \in \MonoidHomMACRoHP {X}{(\MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {\FinMACRoFDI {N}}{\mathrm{PadicAlgCl} (x)})}$. Let $F$ be a function from elements $i_{4}$ of $I$ to linear maps of the set of carriers of $V (i_{4})$ and the set of functions from elements of $\FinMACRoFDI {N}$ to fixed algebraic closures of $x$ such that $\IsEigenQuotientMACRoBHM {\rho}{T}{\theta}{r}{F}$. Assume that $\OverGpMACRoCFV {X}{x}$. Assume that for all elements $i_{3}$ of $I$, $\IsCrystallineinMACRoFCK {\rho (i_{3})}$. Let $\gamma$ be an algebra homomorphism of the set of fixed algebraic closures of $x$ and the set of number complexes ['\'\\\'p-adic_Adj\\\'\''] of $x$. Let $u$ be an integer. Let $a \in \GpMACRoFEY {x}$ such that for all fixed algebraic closures $x_{5}$ of $x$, $\gamma (x_{5})$ is equal to the coercion of $a (x_{5})$. Assume that for all elements $i_{2}$ of $I$, for all elements $s_{1}$ of $S$, for all elements $g$ of $X$, $T (i_{2} , s_{1})$ commutes with $\rho (i_{2} , g)$. Assume that for all elements $i_{1}$ of $I$, for all elements $s$ and $t$ of $S$, $T (i_{1} , s)$ commutes with $T (i_{1} , t)$. Assume that $\jumpMACRoECS {\matrixRepMACRoEGO {r}}{\gamma}{u}= 0$. Then there exists an element $c$ of $\FinsuppMACRoDN {\mathrm{List} (S)}{\mathrm{PadicAlgCl} (x)}$, such that $\EqtMACRoDE {\mathrm{Finsupp\_\_sum} (c , \lambda L . \lambda z . z \times \mathrm{List\_\_prod} (\mathrm{List\_\_map} (\FunctioncompMACRoEM {a}{\theta}, L)))}{1}$ and for all elements $i$ of $I$, for all tensor products $y$ of the set of number complexes ['\'\\\'\\\\\\\'\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'p-adic_Adj\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\'\\\\\\\'\\\'\''] of $x$ and the set of carriers of $V (i)$, if $\HasHodgeTateWeightinMACRoFCH {\rho (i)}{u}{y}$, then $\EqtMACRoDE {\mathrm{Finsupp\_\_sum\_\_app} (c , \lambda L_{2} . \lambda z_{3} . \HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {z_{3}}{\mathrm{LinearMap\_\_baseChange\_\_in\_10\_11} (\mathrm{PadicComplex} (x), \mathrm{ModuleCat\_\_Algebra\_\_instModuleCarrier} , \mathrm{ModuleCat\_\_Algebra\_\_instModuleCarrier} , \mathrm{LinearMap\_\_restrictScalars\_\_in\_9\_10} (\PadicIntMACRoFDG {x}, \mathrm{ModuleCat\_\_Algebra\_\_instModuleCarrier} , \mathrm{ModuleCat\_\_Algebra\_\_instModuleCarrier} , \mathrm{List\_\_prod} (\mathrm{List\_\_map} (T (i), L_{2}))))}, y)}{0}$.
Let θ cut r out of crystalline ρ_i (toGp Γ open, the T_s commuting with each other and with Γ), and η = γθ. If u is no jump of r at γ, some polynomial c with c(η) = 1 has c(T) killing the weight u vectors (`HasHodgeTateWeight`) of each ℂ_ℓ ⊗ V_i.
Proof. By mem_hodgeTateMaps_of_span, nhds_one_le_map_nearOne, IsCrystalline.isPotCrys, jump_eq_zero_iff, IsPotCrys.exists_isHodgeTateFrame, tate_eq_zero_of_chi_twist, Filter.Eventually.all, hodgeTateMaps.pull, exists_words_comb_eq_zero, hodgeTateMaps, tensorAction
and 13 lemmas of Mathlib Module.forall_dual_apply_eq_zero_iff, LinearMap.ext, SMulCommClass.symm, Filter.Eventually.mono, Commute.list_prod_left, LinearMap.finsupp_sum_apply, neg_inj, Finset.smul_sum, Algebra.smul_def, Commute.eq, Module.Basis.ext, TensorProduct.exists_sum_tmul_eq, IsLocalization.isAlgebraic

Lib.Shimura.GoodReduction.ThetaOrthogonality

Definition 40.6src/Lib/Shimura/GoodReduction/ThetaOrthogonality.lean: torsionPoints
Let $V$ be a set. Let $L$ be a submodule of $V$ over $\Integer$. Then $\torsionPointsMACRoEYB {L}$ is a submodule of $V$ over $\Integer$ defined as the copy of the preimage of $\mathrm{Submodule\_\_mkQ} (L)$ and the torsion of $\HasQuotientQuotientMACRoHI {V}{L}$ and $\SubtypeSet {x_{1}}{(\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {\mathrm{nonZeroDivisors} (\Natural)}{x_{1}})}$ and $\SubtypeSet {a}{\mathrm{exists} (\Natural , \lambda n . \mathrm{and} (\mathrm{Neq} (n , 0), \MembershipmemMACRoDQ {L}{(\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {n}{a})}))}$.
The ℤ-submodule of the vectors a of V with na in L for some n ≠ 0: the preimage in V of the torsion subgroup of V/L.
Structure 40.7src/Lib/Shimura/GoodReduction/ThetaOrthogonality.lean: IsAmpleType
Let $V$ be a set. Let $L$ be a submodule of $V$ over $\Integer$. Let $B \in \LatticeFormMACRoBRC {L}$. Let $\chi$ be a function from $V$ to $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Then $\IsAmpleTypeMACRoBEK {B}{\chi}$ is a proposition.
(L, B, χ) is an Appell–Humbert datum with positive Riemann form, and χ has finite order on L: χⁿ = 1 there for some n ≠ 0.
Theorem 40.8src/Lib/Shimura/GoodReduction/ThetaOrthogonality.lean: IsAmpleType.isAppellHumbert
Let $V$ be a finite-dimensional complex space. Let $L$ be a submodule of $V$ over $\Integer$. Let $B \in \LatticeFormMACRoBRC {L}$. Let $\chi$ be a function from $V$ to $\ComplexMACRoAM$ such that $\IsAmpleTypeMACRoBEK {B}{\chi}$. Then $\IsAppellHumbertMACRoBEN {B}{\chi}$.
Proof. By IsAmpleType. ∎

Theorem 40.10src/Lib/Shimura/GoodReduction/ThetaOrthogonality.lean: IsAmpleType.finite_order
Let $V$ be a finite-dimensional complex space. Let $L$ be a submodule of $V$ over $\Integer$. Let $B \in \LatticeFormMACRoBRC {L}$. Let $\chi$ be a function from $V$ to $\ComplexMACRoAM$ such that $\IsAmpleTypeMACRoBEK {B}{\chi}$. Then there exists $n \neq 0$ in $\Natural$, such that for all elements $l$ of $V$, if $l$ is an element of $L$, then $\chi (l)^ {n}$ is equal to $1$.
Proof. By Lean's tactics, without named lemmas. ∎
Definition 40.11src/Lib/Shimura/GoodReduction/ThetaOrthogonality.lean: LatticeForm.sizeAt
Let $V$ be a set. Let $L$ be a submodule of $V$ over $\Integer$. Let $v$ be a valuation of $\ComplexMACRoAM$ and $\NNRealMACRoCF$. Let $B \in \LatticeFormMACRoBRC {L}$. Let $f$ be a function from $V$ to $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Let $x_{1} \in \SubmoduleElemMACRoFFC {\torsionPointsMACRoEYB {L}}$. Then $\LatticeFormsizeAtMACRoBRE {v}{B}{f}{x_{1}}$ is an element of $\NNRealMACRoCF$ defined as $v (\mathrm{RealComplexForm\_\_thetaShift\_\_app} (B , x_{1} , f , 0))$.
The size of f at a vector x torsion modulo L: the valuation v of the value at 0 of the translate `RealComplexForm.thetaShift` of f by x.
Definition 40.12src/Lib/Shimura/GoodReduction/ThetaOrthogonality.lean: LatticeForm.thetaNorm
Let $V$ be a set. Let $L$ be a submodule of $V$ over $\Integer$. Let $B \in \LatticeFormMACRoBRC {L}$. Let $v$ be a valuation of $\ComplexMACRoAM$ and $\NNRealMACRoCF$. Let $f$ be a function from $V$ to $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Then $\LatticeFormthetaNormMACRoBRG {B}{v}{f}$ is an element of $\NNRealMACRoCF$ defined as the supremum of $\LatticeFormsizeAtpartMACRoFVX {v}{B}{f}$.
The norm ‖f‖: the supremum of the size `LatticeForm.sizeAt` of f over all vectors torsion modulo L.
Definition 40.13src/Lib/Shimura/GoodReduction/ThetaOrthogonality.lean: LatticeForm.sizes
Let $V$ be a set. Let $L$ be a submodule of $V$ over $\Integer$. Let $v$ be a valuation of $\ComplexMACRoAM$ and $\NNRealMACRoCF$. Let $B \in \LatticeFormMACRoBRC {L}$. Then $\LatticeFormsizesMACRoBRF {v}{B}$ is an element of the power set of the set of functions from $\SubmoduleElemMACRoFFC {\torsionPointsMACRoEYB {L}}$ to $\NNRealMACRoCF$.
The sizes of degree B: the functions `LatticeForm.sizeAt` of theta functions f for B and some multiplier χ satisfying `IsAmpleType`.
Definition 40.14src/Lib/Shimura/GoodReduction/ThetaOrthogonality.lean: HasMultiplicativeNorm
Let $V$ be a set. Let $v$ be a valuation of $\ComplexMACRoAM$ and $\NNRealMACRoCF$. Let $L$ be a submodule of $V$ over $\Integer$. Then $\HasMultiplicativeNormMACRoAYE {v}{L}$ is a proposition defined as the proposition that for all elements $B$ and $C$ of $\LatticeFormMACRoBRC {L}$, for all functions $\phi$ and $\psi$ from $\SubmoduleElemMACRoFFC {\torsionPointsMACRoEYB {L}}$ to $\NNRealMACRoCF$, if $\phi$ is an element of $\LatticeFormsizesMACRoBRF {v}{B}$, then if $\psi$ is an element of $\LatticeFormsizesMACRoBRF {v}{C}$, then the supremum of $\phi \times \psi$ is equal to the product of the supremum of $\phi$ and the supremum of $\psi$.
Good reduction of V/L at v: for all polarisations B, C and twisted theta functions f, g of these degrees, the sup norm of fg is the product of the norms.
Structure 40.15src/Lib/Shimura/GoodReduction/ThetaOrthogonality.lean: HasGoodReduction
Let $V$ be a set. Let $L$ be a submodule of $V$ over $\Integer$. Let $B \in \LatticeFormMACRoBRC {L}$. Let $\chi$ be a function from $V$ to $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Let $v$ be a valuation of $\ComplexMACRoAM$ and $\NNRealMACRoCF$. Let $r \in \Natural$. Then $\HasGoodReductionMACRoAXP {B}{\chi}{v}{r}$ is a proposition.
The conditions of `IsAmpleType`, with NL^∨ ⊆ L for some natural N with v(N) = 1, r > 1 with v(r) = 1, and multiplicative sup norm (`HasMultiplicativeNorm`).

Definition 40.21src/Lib/Shimura/GoodReduction/ThetaOrthogonality.lean: LatticeForm.HasShiftBound
Let $V$ be a set. Let $L$ be a submodule of $V$ over $\Integer$. Let $v$ be a valuation of $\ComplexMACRoAM$ and $\NNRealMACRoCF$. Let $B \in \LatticeFormMACRoBRC {L}$. Let $w \in \SubmoduleElemMACRoFFC {\torsionPointsMACRoEYB {L}}$. Let $C \in \NNRealMACRoCF$. Then $\LatticeFormHasShiftBoundMACRoBRD {v}{B}{w}{C}$ is a proposition defined as the proposition that for all functions $\phi$ from $\SubmoduleElemMACRoFFC {\torsionPointsMACRoEYB {L}}$ to $\NNRealMACRoCF$, if $\phi$ is an element of $\LatticeFormsizesMACRoBRF {v}{B}$, then for all elements $y$ of $\SubmoduleElemMACRoFFC {\torsionPointsMACRoEYB {L}}$, $\phi (y)^ {2}$ is less than or equal to the product of $C$ and the supremum of the function that maps $x_{1}$ to $\phi (x_{1})\times \phi (x_{1} + w)$.
The torsion vector w has defect at most C in degree B: every size φ of degree B satisfies φ(y)² ≤ C sup_x φ(x) φ(x + w) for all y.

Proof. By LatticeForm.sizeAt, LatticeForm. ∎

Theorem 40.41src/Lib/Shimura/GoodReduction/ThetaOrthogonality.lean: IsAppellHumbert.sum_shift_mul_shift
Let $V$ be a finite-dimensional complex space. Let $L$ be a submodule of $V$ over $\Integer$ such that $\IsFullRankLatticeMACRoBIE {L}$. Let $B \in \LatticeFormMACRoBRC {L}$. Let $\chi$ be a function from $V$ to $\ComplexMACRoAM$ such that $\IsAppellHumbertMACRoBEN {B}{\chi}$. Assume that $\IsPosRiemannMACRoBLY {B}$. Let $T$ be a finite type. Let $a$ be a function from $T$ to $\SubmoduleElemMACRoFFC {\RealComplexFormdualLatticeMACRoCOB {B}}$ such that for all elements $t$ of $\SubmoduleElemMACRoFFC {\RealComplexFormdualLatticeMACRoCOB {B}}$, there exists an equivalence $e$ from $T$ to $T$, such that for all elements $s_{1}$ of $T$, $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {L}{t + a (s_{1})- a (e (s_{1}))}$. Let B be positive, a_s (s ∈ T finite) dual lattice points that each dual translation permutes modulo L, and h = ⌊√[L^∨ : L]⌋. Theta functions f, g satisfy h·Σ_s (U_{−a_s}g)(z)·(U_{a_s}f)(w) = |T|·f(z)g(w), with U = `RealComplexForm.thetaShift`. Known result: Schur orthogonality for the theta group of a polarised complex torus.
Proof. By IsAppellHumbert.schur_lemma, IsAppellHumbert.thetaShift_add_mem, IsAppellHumbert.thetaShift_thetaShift, IsAppellHumbert.thetaShift_mem_of_dual, IsAppellHumbert.thetaShiftEnd, RealComplexForm.thetaShift, IsAppellHumbert.finrank_sq_eq_relIndex

. ∎
Theorem 40.42src/Lib/Shimura/GoodReduction/ThetaOrthogonality.lean: IsAppellHumbert.exists_proportional_orbit_lattice
Let $V$ be a finite-dimensional complex space. Let $L$ be a submodule of $V$ over $\Integer$ such that $\IsFullRankLatticeMACRoBIE {L}$. Let $B \in \LatticeFormMACRoBRC {L}$. Let $\chi$ and $c$ be functions from $V$ to $\ComplexMACRoAM$ such that $\IsAppellHumbertMACRoBEN {B}{\chi}$. Assume that $\IsPosRiemannMACRoBLY {B}$. Let $O$ be a commutative ring such that $O$ is an integral domain, $O$ is a valuation ring, $\ComplexMACRoAM$ is an algebra over $O$ and $\mathrm{IsFractionRing} (O , \ComplexMACRoAM)$. Let $I$ and $J$ be finite sets. Let $f$ be a function from $I$ to $\SubmoduleElemMACRoFFC {(\RealComplexFormthetaSpaceMACRoCOG {\chi}{B})}$. Let $g$ be a function from $J$ to $\SubmoduleElemMACRoFFC {(\RealComplexFormthetaSpaceMACRoCOG {\chi}{B})}$. Let $i \in I$. Let $k \in J$. Let $N$ be a natural number such that $N$ is a unit and for all elements $a_{2}$ of $V$, if $a_{2}$ is an element of $\RealComplexFormdualLatticeMACRoCOB {B}$, then $\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {N}{a_{2}}$ is an element of $L$. Assume that $f (i)\neq 0$. Assume that $g (k)\neq 0$. Assume that for all elements $a_{1}$ of $V$, if $a_{1}$ is an element of $\RealComplexFormdualLatticeMACRoCOB {B}$, then there exists a unit $e$ of $O$, such that $\AlgebraalgebraMapappMACRoFDX {\UnitsvalMACRoFDS {e}}$ is equal to $c (a_{1})\times c (\NegnegMACRoFV {a_{1}})$. Then there exists a complex $\gamma \neq 0$, such that for all submodules $M$ of the set of functions from $V$ to $\ComplexMACRoAM$ over $O$, if for all elements $a$ of $V$, if $a$ is an element of $\RealComplexFormdualLatticeMACRoCOB {B}$, then for all functions $x_{17}$ from $V$ to $\ComplexMACRoAM$, if $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {M}{x_{17}}$, then $\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {c (a)}{\mathrm{RealComplexForm\_\_thetaShift\_\_app} (B , a , x_{17})}$ is an element of $M$, then for all elements $k_{1}$ of $J$, $g (k_{1})$ is an element of $M$, if and only if for all elements $i_{1}$ of $I$, $\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {\gamma}{f (i_{1})}$ is an element of $M$.
Let B be positive, N·L^∨ ⊂ L with N and all c(a)c(−a) units of the valuation ring O. For finite non-zero theta families f_i, g_k and some γ ≠ 0, an O-module stable under the c(a)U_a (a ∈ L^∨) contains all g_k iff all γf_i.
Proof. By IsAppellHumbert.sum_shift_mul_shift, IsAppellHumbert.thetaShiftEnd, RealComplexForm.thetaShift, IsAppellHumbert.finrank_sq_eq_relIndex, RealComplexForm.dualLattice, RealComplexForm.thetaSpace
and 18 lemmas of Mathlib QuotientAddGroup.eq_iff_sub_mem, Submodule.sum_smul_mem, Function.mt, algebraMap.coe_eq_zero_iff, smul_inv_smul₀, isUnit_of_dvd_unit, mul_assoc, inv_mul_eq_div, Units.ne_zero, nsmul_mem, Finite.exists_max, IsUnit.pow, Submodule.smul_mem_iff_of_isUnit, Dvd.dvd.natCast, Function.ne_iff, Algebra.smul_def, QuotientAddGroup.eq_zero_iff, card_dvd_exponent_nsmul_rank'
. ∎

Lib.Shimura.GoodReduction.HermitianAction

Structure 40.43src/Lib/Shimura/GoodReduction/HermitianAction.lean: IsHermitianAction
Let $V$ and $R$ be sets. Let $L$ be a submodule of $V$ over $\Integer$. Let $B \in \LatticeFormMACRoBRC {L}$. Let $F$ be a ring homomorphism from $R$ to the set of continuous linear maps of $V$ and $V$. Let $\beta \in R$. Then $\IsHermitianActionMACRoBIQ {L}{B}{F}{\beta}$ is a proposition.
B is a positive definite hermitian form with Riemann form integral on L, the action F of R preserves L with ā adjoint to a, and β̄ = 1 − β.
Theorem 40.44src/Lib/Shimura/GoodReduction/HermitianAction.lean: IsHermitianAction.map_mem
Let $V$ be a finite-dimensional complex space. Let $R$ be a commutative ring such that $R$ is a star ring. Let $L$ be a submodule of $V$ over $\Integer$. Let $B \in \LatticeFormMACRoBRC {L}$. Let $l \in V$ such that $l$ is an element of $L$. Then $\DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {F}{a}$ is an element of $L$.
Proof. By Lean's tactics, without named lemmas. ∎

Theorem 40.67src/Lib/Shimura/GoodReduction/HermitianAction.lean: IsHermitianAction.normShift_sum_eq_char_smul
Let $V$ be a finite-dimensional complex space. Let $R$ be a commutative ring such that $R$ is a star ring. Let $L$ be a submodule of $V$ over $\Integer$. Let $B \in \LatticeFormMACRoBRC {L}$. Let $F$ be a ring homomorphism from $R$ to the set of continuous linear maps of $V$ and $V$. Let $\beta , a \in R$. Assume that $R$ is an integral domain. Assume that $\mathrm{Module\_\_Finite} (\Integer , R)$. Let $f$ and $s$ be functions from $V$ to $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Assume that $\IsHermitianActionMACRoBIQ {L}{B}{F}{\beta}$. Assume that $a$ is nonzero. Assume that $\IsFullRankLatticeMACRoBIE {L}$. Assume that for all elements $l$ of $V$, if $\MembershipmemMACRoDQ {L}{l}$, then $\EqtMACRoDE {\mathrm{IsHermitianAction\_\_normShift\_\_app} (h_{2} , \DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {F}{a}, f)}{f}$. Assume that $\EqtMACRoDE {s}{\FinsetSum {\FinsetunivMACRoDZ}{k}{x_{12}}{(\HSMulhSMulappMACRoJP {\xi}{\mathrm{IsHermitianAction\_\_normShift\_\_app} (h_{2} , \DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {F}{a}, f)}{x_{12}})}}$. Then $\mathrm{IsHermitianAction\_\_normShift\_\_app} (h_{2} , \DFunLikecoeappMACRoHM {F}{a}, s)$ is equal to $\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {\xi}{s}$.
Let f be fixed by the normalised translations by aL, ξ a character of the finite kernel of a on V/L, and s = ∑_k ξ(−k)·(translate of f by ak). If ay ∈ L, the normalised translation by ay multiplies s by ξ(y).

and 8 lemmas of Mathlib QuotientAddGroup.mk_out_eq_add, add_assoc, AddChar.map_add_eq_mul, smul_smul, map_sum, Finset.smul_sum, AddMemClass.add_mem, Fintype.sum_equiv
. ∎

Theorem 40.68src/Lib/Shimura/GoodReduction/GaussUnfolding.lean: IsHermitianAction.gauss_sum_pow_four_real
Let $V$ be a finite-dimensional complex space. Let $R$ be a commutative ring such that $R$ is a star ring. Let $L$ be a submodule of $V$ over $\Integer$. Let $B \in \LatticeFormMACRoBRC {L}$. Let $F$ be a ring homomorphism from $R$ to the set of continuous linear maps of $V$ and $V$. Let $\beta , \kappa , r \in R$ such that $\IsHermitianActionMACRoBIQ {L}{B}{F}{\beta}$. Then $\EqtMACRoDE {(\FinsetSum {\FinsetunivMACRoDZ}{z}{\mathrm{starRingEnd\_\_app} (\ComplexMACRoAM , q (z))})^ {4}}{(\FinsetSum {\FinsetunivMACRoDZ}{z_{1}}{q (z_{1})})^ {4}}$.
Let κ ≠ 0 and r lie in R, and let q be a function on κ⁻¹L/L with q(z) = exp B(x, rx) for every lift x of z. Then the fourth power of the Gauss sum ∑ q(z) is real.

and 20 lemmas of Mathlib Function.Surjective.comp_left, Function.Surjective.forall, QuotientAddGroup.mk_surjective, Finite.surjective_iff_bijective, Complex.exp_nsmul, Fintype.sum_pow, NeZero.one_le, Nat.sum_four_squares, sq, pow_mul, QuotientAddGroup.forall_mk, Finset.sum_bijective, Fin.sum_univ_four, sub_nsmul, pow_sub_one_mul, Complex.exp_sum, Complex.inv_eq_conj, Complex.norm_eq_one_of_pow_eq_one, SemigroupAction.mul_smul, inv_eq_of_mul_eq_one_left
. ∎
Theorem 40.69src/Lib/Shimura/GoodReduction/GaussUnfolding.lean: IsHermitianAction.exp_eq_of_mem_lattice
Let $V$ be a finite-dimensional complex space. Let $R$ be a commutative ring such that $R$ is a star ring. Let $L$ be a submodule of $V$ over $\Integer$. Let $B \in \LatticeFormMACRoBRC {L}$. Definition 40.90src/Lib/Shimura/ThetaNormality/NormalGeneration.lean: formSpan

Definition 40.92src/Lib/Shimura/ThetaNormality/NormalGeneration.lean: IsIntegralCoords
Let $V$ be a set. Let $v$ be a valuation of $\ComplexMACRoAM$ and $\NNRealMACRoCF$. Let $w$ be a function from $V$ to $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Then $\IsIntegralCoordsMACRoBJG {w}{p}$ is a proposition defined as the proposition that there exists a complex $s$, such that for all elements $j$ of $V$, $p (j)$ is equal to $\frac{ w (j)}{s}$ and there exists an element $c$ of $V$, such that $v (p (c))$ is equal to $1$.
p is a primitive integral scaling of w: p_j = w_j/s for one complex s and all j, and some coordinate p_c is a unit of the valuation ring.

Theorem 40.105src/Lib/Shimura/ThetaNormality/NormalGeneration.lean: HasGoodReduction.normBall_pow_pow

Proof. By Lean's tactics, without named lemmas. ∎

Definition 40.123src/Lib/Shimura/ThetaModuli/ThetaTorus.lean: PeriodMap.latticeOver
Let $E$ and $V$ be sets. Let $D \in \PELMACRoCFY {E}$. Let $g \in \MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {\PELIndexMACRoCGA {D}}{\udfMACRoFEH {E}}$. Let $x_{2}$ be a matrix of $\PELIndexMACRoCGA {D}$ and $\ComplexMACRoAM$. Let $x_{4} \in \PeriodMapMACRoCJX {D}{g}{x_{2}}{V}$. Let $S$ be a subgroup of $\AdelicTorsionMACRoKG {E}{\PELIndexMACRoCGA {D}}$. Then $\PeriodMaplatticeOverMACRoCKE {x_{4}}{S}$ is a submodule of $V$ over $\Integer$ defined as $\classLatticeMACRoDNE {g}{S}{\PeriodMapQMACRoCJZ {x_{4}}}$.
For a subgroup S of the torsion module, the image in V under the period map of the vectors of E^n whose class with respect to g lies in S.
Definition 40.124src/Lib/Shimura/ThetaModuli/ThetaTorus.lean: PeriodMap.periodLattice

The period lattice in V: the image under the period map of the vectors y in E^n with g⁻¹y integral.

The function on V sending u to exp(πi·e(β₁u, u)), where e is the Riemann form of B and β₁ = |T|β − ⌊|T|/2⌋.

Theorem 40.173src/Lib/Shimura/Transfer/MarkedPairs.lean: ratConj_eigenReflection
Let $E$ be a field. Let $n$ be a finite type. Assume that $\CMBaseMACRoPE {E}$. Let $X$ be a proposition. Let $\mu \in n$. Let $y , z \in \MatrixGeneralLinearGroupMACRoFDH {n}{E}$. Then $\ratConjMACRoEOL {z}{(\eigenReflectionMACRoDRU {X}{y}{\mu})}$ is equal to $\eigenReflectionMACRoDRU {X}{(z \times y \times \InvinvMACRoFW {z})}{\mu}$.
For y, z in GL_n(E), Φ_{v₀}(z)·J_μ(y)·Φ_{v₀}(z)⁻¹ = J_μ(zyz⁻¹), where J_μ(y) is the matrix `eigenReflection` ε y μ, a polynomial in Φ_{v₀}(y), with the same ε and root index μ on both sides.

Theorem 40.200src/Lib/Shimura/ThetaGroup/ComplexComparison.lean: lift_commutator_iff_weil_pairing

Theorem 40.212src/Lib/Shimura/Deformation/DrinfeldLifting.lean: mem_powers_card_of_isLinearMap_pow
Let $k$ and $F$ be fields such that $\mathrm{IsAlgClosed} (k)$. Let $i \in \Natural$. Assume that $F$ is finite. Assume that $k$ is an algebra over $F$. Assume that the function that maps $x_{3}$ to $x_{3} ^ {i}$ is a linear map. Then $i$ is an element of the power of the cardinality of $F$.
If `x ↦ x ^ i` is `F`-linear on an algebraically closed extension of a field `F` with `q` elements, then `i` is a power of `q`: the fixed points of `x ^ (q i)` form an `F`-space.

. ∎

Then $\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {\mathrm{ringChar} (k)}{y}$ is equal to $0$ and for all elements $\psi$ of $\RingHomMACRoHO {k}{\mathrm{TrivSqZeroExt} (k , k)}$, if $\ThetaObjectbaseHomMACRoDCA {x_{3}}$ is equal to $\RingHomcompMACRoFDN {\psi}{\ThetaObjectbaseHomMACRoDCA {x_{0}}}$, then for all integers $b$, for all elements $c$ of $\NumberFieldRingOfIntegersMACRoFDD {E}$, if $\mathrm{ThetaObject\_\_baseHom\_\_app} (x_{0} , \PELubMACRoCGE {D}- b)$ is equal to $0$, then if $\mathrm{ThetaObject\_\_baseHom\_\_app} (x_{0} , c)$ is equal to $0$, then $\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {((\mathrm{Star\_\_star} (\PELubMACRoCGE {D})- b)\times c)}{y}$ is equal to $0$.
Proof. By IsInfinitesimal.smul, specOver, IsInfinitesimal, ThetaObject.exists_tangent_log, eq_zero_iff_factors_zero_section, hom_ext_of_cover, ThetaObject.smoothOfRelativeDimension, natCast_eq_zero_of_over_charP, LineBundle.one, IsInfinitesimal.nsmul_injective_and_eq_zero, exists_isSqZeroExt_mod_eps
and 2 lemmas of Mathlib CategoryTheory.Over.OverMorphism.ext, SemigroupAction.mul_smul
. ∎
Theorem 40.223src/Lib/Shimura/Deformation/DrinfeldLifting.lean: drinfeld_lift
Let $E$ and $k$ be fields such that $\CMBaseMACRoPE {E}$. Let $D \in \PELMACRoCFY {E}$. Let $Z$ be a scheme. Let $\sigma$ be a homomorphism of $Z$ and the spectrum of $\mathrm{CommRingCat\_\_of} (\NumberFieldRingOfIntegersMACRoFDD {E})$. Let $Y \in \ThetaFamilyMACRoDAY {D}{Z}{\sigma}$. Let $x_{0} \in \ThetaObjectMACRoDBM {Y}{\mathrm{AlgebraicGeometry\_\_Spec} (\mathrm{CommRingCat\_\_of} (k))}$. Let $x_{3} , x' \in \ThetaObjectMACRoDBM {Y}{\mathrm{AlgebraicGeometry\_\_Spec} (\mathrm{CommRingCat\_\_of} (\mathrm{TrivSqZeroExt} (k , k)))}$ such that $\ThetaObjectIsPullbackAlongMACRoDBQ {\sqZeroReductionMACRoEUD {k}{k}}{x_{0}}{x_{3}}$ and $\ThetaObjectIsPullbackAlongMACRoDBQ {\sqZeroReductionMACRoEUD {k}{k}}{x_{0}}{x'}$. Let $c \in \NumberFieldRingOfIntegersMACRoFDD {E}$ such that for all elements $T_{1}$ of $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Over}$ applied to the spectrum of $\mathrm{CommRingCat\_\_of}$ applied to the set of trivial square zero extensions of $k$ and $k$, for all homomorphisms $y_{1}$ of $T_{1}$ and $\ThetaObjectgroupSchemeMACRoDCB {x'}$, if $\AgreeModEpsMACRoKH {\mathrm{hq'}}{\mathrm{hq'}}{T_{1}}{y_{1}}{0}$, then $\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {c}{y_{1}}$ is equal to $0$. Then there exists a homomorphism $F$ of $\ThetaObjectgroupSchemeMACRoDCB {x_{3}}$ and $\ThetaObjectgroupSchemeMACRoDCB {x'}$, such that $\IsAdditiveMACRoBEA {F}$, the composition of $\ThetaObjectIsPullbackAlongschemeMapMACRoDBR {\mathrm{hq}}$ and $F$ is equal to $\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {c}{\ThetaObjectIsPullbackAlongschemeMapMACRoDBR {\mathrm{hq'}}}$ and for all elements $T$ of $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Over}$ applied to the spectrum of $\mathrm{CommRingCat\_\_of}$ applied to the set of trivial square zero extensions of $k$ and $k$, for all homomorphisms $y'$ of $T$ and $\ThetaObjectgroupSchemeMACRoDCB {x'}$, for all homomorphisms $y$ of $T$ and $\ThetaObjectgroupSchemeMACRoDCB {x_{3}}$, if $\AgreeModEpsMACRoKH {\mathrm{hq'}}{\mathrm{hq}}{T}{y'}{y}$, then the composition of $y$ and $F$ is equal to $\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {c}{y'}$.
Drinfeld's lifting lemma. Let x, x' deform x₀ over k[ε], and let c in O_E kill all infinitesimal points of A_x'. Then some homomorphism F: A_x → A_x' reduces to c, and F(y) = c·y' whenever y and y' agree modulo ε.
Proof. By ThetaObject.IsPullbackAlong, exists_isEtaleChart, ThetaObject.IsPullbackAlong.comp_toFamily, IsGroupBaseChange.pointEquiv, ThetaObject.toFamily, AgreeModEps, ThetaFamily.relative_dim, ThetaObject.isGroupBaseChange, exists_isSqZeroExt_mod_eps, exists_hom_of_rel
and 6 lemmas of Mathlib Function.Surjective.comp_left, CategoryTheory.Over.OverMorphism.ext, AlgebraicGeometry.Scheme.exists_affine_mem_range_and_range_subset, CategoryTheory.Over.w, CategoryTheory.Over.Hom.w, AlgebraicGeometry.Scheme.zariskiPrecoverage_le_fpqcPrecoverage
. ∎

Theorem 40.224src/Lib/Shimura/Extension/ArcExtension.lean: exists_section_of_hasThetaCoords
Let $V$ be a finite-dimensional complex space. Let $I$ and $J$ be sets. Let $O$ be a commutative ring such that $\ValuativeArcMACRoDFY {O}$. Let $A$ and $A'$ be elements of $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Over}$ applied to the spectrum of $\mathrm{CommRingCat\_\_of} (O)$ such that $\AddObjMACRoKB {A'}$. Assume that $\IsPosRiemannMACRoBLY {B}$. Assume that for all elements $l_{4}$ of $V$, if $l_{4}$ is an element of $\UniformisationLMACRoDFK {u}$, then $s' (l_{4})$ is equal to $0$. Assume that the homomorphism of $A'$ is proper. Let $M \in \LineBundleMACRoCBH {\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Over\_\_left} (A')}{J}$ such that $\LineBundleIsProjEmbeddingMACRoCBN {M}{\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Over\_\_hom} (A')}$. Let $\pi$ be a homomorphism of $A'$ and $A$ such that $\IsAdditiveMACRoBEA {\pi}$. Let $\nu$ be a homomorphism of the left of $A'$ and the left of $A'$. Assume that the exponentiation of $n$ to $2$ is equal to $m$. Assume that for all elements $z_{2}$ of $V$, the composition of $s' (z_{2})$ and $\pi$ is equal to $\mathrm{Uniformisation\_\_expMap\_\_app} (u , z_{2})$. Assume that $\HasThetaCoordsMACRoAYU {\UniformisationLMACRoDFK {u}}{\atGenericpartMACRoFXF {s'}}{M}{B}{\chi}{f}$. Assume that the left of $\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {n}{\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_CategoryStruct\_\_id} (A')}$ is equal to $\nu$. Assume that $\LineBundlepicClassMACRoCBY {(\LineBundlemulMACRoCBU {(\LineBundlepullMACRoCCA {(\LineBundlepullMACRoCCA {N}{\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Over\_\_Hom\_\_left} (\pi)})}{\nu})}{(\LineBundlepowMACRoCBZ {M}{m})})}$ is equal to $\LineBundlepicClassMACRoCBY {(\LineBundlemulMACRoCBU {(\LineBundlepullMACRoCCA {M}{\nu})}{(\LineBundlepowMACRoCBZ {(\LineBundlepullMACRoCCA {N}{\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Over\_\_Hom\_\_left} (\pi)})}{m})})}$. Then there exists a homomorphism $e$ of $A$ and $A'$, such that for all elements $z$ of $V$, the composition of $\mathrm{Uniformisation\_\_expMap\_\_app} (u , z)$ and $e$ is equal to $s' (z)$, $\LineBundlepicClassMACRoCBY {(\LineBundlepullMACRoCCA {M}{\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Over\_\_Hom\_\_left} (e)})}$ is equal to $\LineBundlepicClassMACRoCBY {N}$ and the composition of $e$ and $\pi$ is equal to the identity morphism of $A$.

Theorem 40.225src/Lib/Shimura/Extension/ArcExtension.lean: exists_hom_of_hasThetaCoords
Let $V$ be a finite-dimensional complex space. Let $I$ and $J$ be sets. Let $O$ be a commutative ring such that $\ValuativeArcMACRoDFY {O}$. Let $A$ and $A'$ be elements of $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Over}$ applied to the spectrum of $\mathrm{CommRingCat\_\_of} (O)$ such that $\AddObjMACRoKB {A'}$. Let $s' \in \AddMonoidHomMACRoIE {V}{\mathrm{Quiver\_\_Hom} (\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_MonoidalCategoryStruct\_\_tensorUnit} (\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Over} (\mathrm{AlgebraicGeometry\_\_Spec} (\mathrm{CommRingCat\_\_of} (O)))), A')}$. Assume that $J$ is finite. Assume that $\IsAbelianSchemeMACRoBDT {A}$. Assume that $I$ is finite. Let $u \in \UniformisationMACRoDFJ {A}{V}$. Let $N \in \LineBundleMACRoCBH {\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Over\_\_left} (A)}{I}$. Let $B \in \LatticeFormMACRoBRC {\UniformisationLMACRoDFK {u}}$. Assume that $\IsPosRiemannMACRoBLY {B}$. Assume that for all elements $l$ of $V$, if $l$ is an element of $\UniformisationLMACRoDFK {u}$, then $s' (l)$ is equal to $0$. Let $n$ and $\mu$ be natural numbers such that $n$ is a unit and for all elements $a_{1}$ of $V$, if $a_{1}$ is an element of $\RealComplexFormdualLatticeMACRoCOB {B}$, then $\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {n}{a_{1}}$ is an element of $\UniformisationLMACRoDFK {u}$ and there exists an end $\Phi$ of $O$ and $\SubmoduleElemMACRoFFC {(\LineBundleHMACRoCBI {N}{O})}$, such that $\IsTranslationLiftMACRoBPF {N}{\mathrm{Uniformisation\_\_expMap\_\_app} (u , a_{1})}{\Phi}$. Assume that the homomorphism of $A'$ is proper. Let $M \in \LineBundleMACRoCBH {\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Over\_\_left} (A')}{J}$ such that $\LineBundleIsProjEmbeddingMACRoCBN {M}{\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Over\_\_hom} (A')}$. Assume that $\LineBundleIsProjEmbeddingMACRoCBN {N}{\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Over\_\_hom} (A)}$. Assume that $\PicParallelogramLawMACRoCKS {\LineBundlepicClassMACRoCBY {N}}$. Assume that $\PicParallelogramLawMACRoCKS {\LineBundlepicClassMACRoCBY {M}}$. Assume that $\HasThetaCoordsMACRoAYU {\UniformisationLMACRoDFK {u}}{\atGenericpartMACRoFXF {s'}}{M}{\HSMulhSMulMACRoEI {\mu}{B}}{\chi ^ {\mu}}{\theta'}$. Let A = V/Λ over O and s': V → A'(O) additive killing Λ, with embeddings N, M obeying the parallelogram law and theta coordinates of positive type (B, χ), resp. (μB, χ^μ). If Λ^∨ maps into K(N) and nΛ^∨ ⊂ Λ, n a unit, then s' comes from a homomorphism ψ with ψ^*[M] = μ[N]. Known result: Homomorphisms and line bundles of abelian schemes extend from the generic fibre.
Proof. By LineBundle.mul, HasThetaCoords.pow, exists_section_of_hasThetaCoords, LineBundle.pow, LineBundle.IsProjEmbedding.mul, exists_isTranslationLift_iff, pull_nsmul_eq_sq_smul, HasThetaCoords.mul, IsSeparatingFamily.hom_ext, mem_dualLattice_nsmul, LineBundle.IsProjEmbedding.of_isPullback, LineBundle.IsProjEmbedding.pow, Pic.ParallelogramLaw.nsmul_class_pairing, LineBundle.pull, Uniformisation.expMap, isAdditive_of_universal, atGeneric, Uniformisation.surjective, HasThetaCoords.pull, isSeparatingFamily_atGeneric, Pic.pull
and 14 lemmas of Mathlib CategoryTheory.AddMonObj.comp_nsmul, Function.Surjective.prodMap, pow_add, CategoryTheory.AddMonObj.comp_add, map_add, IsUnit.mul, mul_nsmul, add_smul, CategoryTheory.IsPullback.flip, CategoryTheory.IsPullback.of_hasPullback, Ideal.sub_mem, Equiv.surjective, map_nsmul, CategoryTheory.CartesianMonoidalCategory.hom_ext
